# IntegralComparisonSequenceSystems import direction

## Reason

The sequence-site module is unclaimed. Its public import of the higher
Sheaf.Paper.IntegralPullbackComposition creates a cycle once the item
IntegralPullbackSystemComparison imports its declared normalized-realization
dependencies (EffectiveQuotientModelDescent, IntegralComparisonRealizationModels),
which import this module. No declaration of the sequence module uses the higher
comparison or composition theorem. The import is replaced by the lower public
modules that the higher import re-exported and that this module and its users
need, including the smallness interfaces whose loss broke
FiniteCounitRestrictionTowers in the reverted C4 packet.

Reviewer correction (2026-10-26): the delivered packet also aliased the four
public definitions integralComparisonSequenceBaseChange, …RingComponent,
…RingMap and …DirectImage to the ambient AmbientPullbackTensor constructions
and rewrote 17 private proofs. That was not forced by the cycle, and it
broke the unchanged downstream module
Sheaf.Paper.CompletedComparisonQuotientRealizationDefs (line 174:
`unfold pullbackTensorSequenceDirect integralComparisonSequenceDirectImage;
rw [hr]` leaves `SheafOfModules.pushforward … = AmbientPullbackTensor.push l E g none`).
The landed module is therefore the published one with only the import block
changed.

## Old

```lean
public import Sheaf.Paper.IntegralPullbackComposition
```

## New

```lean
public import Sheaf.Paper.DerivedPullbackTensorHom
public import Sheaf.Paper.IntegralFinitePullbackComposition
public import Sheaf.Paper.IntegralSystemReductionFullyFaithful
public import Sheaf.Paper.IntegralDerivedOperationsOnSlices
public import Sheaf.CategoryTheory.RingedSiteDualityMaps
```

All other lines (every definition body, proof, option and the private import of
AmbientPullbackTensorCoherence) are byte-identical to the published module.
No signature, definition body, instance or attribute changes. Only
IntegralPullbackComposition (and what only it re-exported) stops being visible
through this module. All 38 direct importers and name users were compiled
against this version by the reviewer. Three fail or run over 60 s identically
against the published build (CompletedPrimitiveCounitComparison: type mismatch
at 377; IntegralInverseImageComparisonComposition: slow; TransportedReductionSystemsNormalized: missing
upstream oleans, compiles with them in the sandbox).

## Users

The following includes the live downstream.py list and the complete downstream
closure with the sandbox import repairs applied, with
claim holders read from the current claim records. No target statement or
underlying construction changes. Direct consumer validation and the previous
failure are discussed in report.md.
